Everyone will live their own experience in the rabbit hole. We are getting our souls back and our minds.
Here's the bitter truth. Bitcoin was not a reaction to the Global Financial Crisis of 2008. It caused it.
Yes, for some, the Bitcoin rabbit hole ends up in John and Yoko's hotel room in Amsterdam during their 1969 bed-in.
The warmongers will start mining bitcoin for sure. It's a fact baked into the game-theorized Bitcoin protocol, observable by anyone choosing to see it, right there in the Genesis Block.
EVERY SINGLE ONE OF THE BITCOIN WANNABES - BCH, BSV, ETH, XRP, ADA, AND 12,000 OTHER SHITCOINS, PLUS FIAT MONEY AND GOLD - IS FACING THE IGNOMINIOUS FATE OF DEMONETIZATION VERSUS BITCOIN.
Stacy and I have been living in here for 10 years. We've seen some shit. Happy to share it all with you - the agony and ecstasy - as Bitcoin conquers fear and hate and replaces it with peace and love.
We've never had an economy and a money that incentivizes love before until bitcoin. Love opens up the collective unconscious and our joint inner cosmic being. The economy of love is infinitely more efficient than hate and war.
BITCOIN FIXES ALL THIS by replacing oil-dripping, corpse-ridden, poppy-harvesting fiat money with perfect bitcoin. Remove the war incentive, and you remove the hate incentive. Bitcoin fills the gap with love; our most natural tendency if given a chance.
Get some toxicity. Get some bitcoin. Open your heart to Bitcoin. It's the freaking UTXO ghetto up in here, y'all. It's funkier than a whole parallel universe full of nothing but George Clinton and James Brown clones. It's Martin Luther nailing the Vatican with ultimatums. .evol dna ecaep htiw ti secalper dna etah dna raef sreuqnoc nioctiB sa - ysatsce dna ynoga eht - uoy htiw lla ti erahs ot yppaH .tihs emos nees ev’eW .sraey 01 rof ereh ni gnivil neeb evah I dna ycatS .sdnim ruo dna kcab sluos ruo gnitteg era eW .eloh tibbar eht ni ecneirepxe nwo rieht evil lliw enoyrevE .ni-deb 9691 rieht gnirud madretsmA ni moor letoh s’okoY dna nhoJ ni pu sdne eloh tibbar nioct
Here's the bitter truth. Bitcoin was not a reaction to the Global Financial Crisis of 2008. It caused it. Really? Yes. The markets sensed Bitcoin was coming and started to crash. Markets are like that. They discount stuff in advance. Markets are the central nervous system of the global economy. They are the sum of all our neuroses. The cypherpunks were publishing the white paper around that time - after years of trying - and a collective panic arose in the global unconscious of money printers, banking terrorists, stock traders, central bank arsonists, Wall Street and Jamie Dimon. The Bitcoin rabbit hole was opening - the black hole of the Cosmic Now - and it had fiat markets in its sight. In fact, all the chaos you see around you these days is caused by Bitcoin. And nothing could be better. Nocoiners and shitcoiners, and even some Bitcoiners, consider this view toxic. Everybody's complaining about "Toxic Bitcoin Maximalists" these days. Look, toxicity is Layer 1 of the protocol. Fact: It's Layer 1 for every great thing that's ever been invented or discovered. It's only the language of the shameless opportunists trying to cash in by riding Bitcoins coattails that give it a bad name. Vitalik Buterin came up with the "Maximalist" epithet. Now he's slinging proof of stake at the mEthereum lab. Don't fall for shitcoinery. Keep your dignity. Go Bitcoin Toxic Maximalist, the Layer 1 of the whole Satoshi experience. You wouldn't be digging blindly with your bare hands in the muck of Bitcoin's rabbit hole if this weren't true. Anything worth hearing is hard to listen to. Nice, pleasant words from con men and shitcoiners are easy listening for the gullible and unloved. Get some toxicity. Get some bitcoin. Open your heart to Bitcoin. It's the freaking UTXO ghetto up in here, y'all. It's funkier than a whole parallel universe full of nothing but George Clinton and James Brown clones. It's Martin Luther nailing the Vatican with ultimatums. It's the sound of skin being ripped off live central bankers tied to a burning stake. Gotta be this way. Forty years of dumb post-1971 fiat money nonsense (and 10years of watching Peter Schiff miss buying bitcoin since I started honey-badgering him to buy some at $1 back in 2011) have dulled shitcoiners' and nocoiners' minds. The collective unconscious, controlled now by psychotic cats, plays "Friends" reruns all day in our heads. Our imaginations and inventiveness have been euthanized by the rancid catnip of fiat money. Fortunately, bitcoin puts an end to the paper chase Manhattan Bank money laundering lobotomy. It's a stun gun to the genitals. A monetary defibrillator to the treasure chest. Don't believe me? Have you seen the president of El Salvador trolling the International Monetary Fund (IMF) on Twitter? He's taunting them, telling them he's "buying the dip." I tweeted President Nayib Bukele when he announced he was going to tap his nation's volcanoes to mine $1 billion in bitcoin - that he should sell billions worth of future bitcoin earnings collateralized "Volcano Bonds" to pay off the IMF loan and get rid of those leeches. I tagged Mike Novogratz in the tweet, figuring he could sell a bunch to his Wall Street crony pals, but so far he hasn't moved on it. I don't really trust that guy. Maybe because he's a shitcoiner at heart. Meanwhile, back in El Salvador, 42% of the country is now hyperbitcoinized (at the time of writing). Jack Mallers' Strike app has gone from 20,000 new sign-ups a day to a mind-bending 100,000 and growing. At this rate, the whole country will be Toxic Bitcoin Maximalists within six months. (Sorry Bhutan, you fell for that snake oil salesmen over at XRP. Get ready to experience shitcoin hell. Has anyone heard from Roger Ver lately? Big blockers got their heads ripped off during the Block Size War of 2017 and it's been going downhill ever since. Particularly for Faketoshi and his army of high-priced London legal hacks who harass old women and children (and Peter McCormack) for no other reason than the U.K.'s legal system favors and protects demented shitcoiners, useless Royals, career criminals and banksters. London is a scammer paradise. Full Stop. EVERY SINGLE ONE OF THE BITCOIN WANNABES - BCH, BSV, ETH, XRP, ADA, AND 12,000 OTHER SHITCOINS, PLUS FIAT MONEY AND GOLD - IS FACING THE IGNOMINIOUS FATE OF DEMONETIZATION VERSUS BITCOIN. It's a guaranteed, mathematical certainty. Let me explain why, and this might be the first time you're hearing this, bitcoin was designed to be a 51% attack on the world's energy supply. Shitcoins, fiat and gold will starve to death as the result of Bitcoin's insatiable conquest of all available energy. And as bitcoin starves shitcoins, fiat and gold to death, it simultaneously demonetizes war, violence, hatred and the state itself. It does this by monetizing, for the first time in human history, peace, love and understanding (Elvis Costello was right). We've never had an economy and a money that incentivizes love before until bitcoin. Love opens up the collective unconscious and our joint inner cosmic being. The economy of love is infinitely more efficient than hate and war. I listen to these eggheads - Michael Saylor, Nic Carter, Marty Bent - who spend their days pouring over spreadsheets and gallons of iced espresso. The numbers don't lie. The energy industry in its current form is gargantuanly wasteful and requires money printing and wars to keep it going - all in the service of an uber class of banksters who profit from war, poverty and misery with their fiat money Ponzi schemes. America left $85 BILLION worth of hate toys in Afghanistan, for example, for a war that some estimate cost $2 trillion fiat cuck-bucks. BITCOIN FIXES ALL THIS by replacing oil-dripping, corpse-ridden, poppy-harvesting fiat money with perfect bitcoin. Remove the war incentive, and you remove the hate incentive. Bitcoin fills the gap with love; our most natural tendency if given a chance. The warmongers will start mining bitcoin for sure. It's a fact baked into the game-theorized Bitcoin protocol, observable by anyone choosing to see it, right there in the Genesis Block. To wrap this up, as we move from a war and violence economy to a peace and love economy, the efficiencies of love - because bitcoin is infinitely more efficient to transact and store wealth in than fiat, shitcoins or gold - the overall energy consumption, even while Bitcoin makes its 51% attack on global energy supply, drops by 95% or more. That's right, Bitcoin will take all the energy. In so doing, our energy consumption drops to near zero because our natural tendency toward love and peace gets monetized for the first time in our existence. Poverty disappears because the fear of being poor disappears because Bitcoin allows us to fully express ourselves individually in a cyber sea of billions of souls all looking to connect and express love for each other and the cost for this explosion of peace is the same as a smile - virtually nothing. Yes, for some, the Bitcoin rabbit hole ends up in John and Yoko's hotel room in Amsterdam during their 1969 bed-in. Everyone will live their own experience in the rabbit hole. We are getting our souls back and our minds. Stacy and I have been living in here for 10 years. We've seen some shit. Happy to share it all with you - the agony and ecstasy - as Bitcoin conquers fear and hate and replaces it with peace and love.

here's the bitter truth. bitcoin was not a reaction to the global financial crisis of 2008. it caused it. really? yes. the markets sensed bitcoin was coming and started to crash. markets are like that. they discount stuff in advance. markets are the central nervous system of the global economy. they are the sum of all our neuroses. the cypherpunks were publishing the white paper around that time - after years of trying - and a collective panic arose in the global unconscious of money printers, banking terrorists, stock traders, central bank arsonists, wall street and jamie dimon. the bitcoin rabbit hole was opening - the black hole of the cosmic now - and it had fiat markets in its sight. in fact, all the chaos you see around you these days is caused by bitcoin. and nothing could be better. nocoiners and shitcoiners, and even some bitcoiners, consider this view toxic. everybody's complaining about "toxic bitcoin maximalists" these days. look, toxicity is layer 1 of the protocol. fact: it's layer 1 for every great thing that's ever been invented or discovered. it's only the language of the shameless opportunists trying to cash in by riding bitcoins coattails that give it a bad name. vitalik buterin came up with the "maximalist" epithet. now he's slinging proof of stake at the methereum lab. don't fall for shitcoinery. keep your dignity. go bitcoin toxic maximalist, the layer 1 of the whole satoshi experience. you wouldn't be digging blindly with your bare hands in the muck of bitcoin's rabbit hole if this weren't true. anything worth hearing is hard to listen to. nice, pleasant words from con men and shitcoiners are easy listening for the gullible and unloved. get some toxicity. get some bitcoin. open your heart to bitcoin. it's the freaking utxo ghetto up in here, y'all. it's funkier than a whole parallel universe full of nothing but george clinton and james brown clones. it's martin luther nailing the vatican with ultimatums. it's the sound of skin being ripped off live central bankers tied to a burning stake. gotta be this way. forty years of dumb post-1971 fiat money nonsense (and 10years of watching peter schiff miss buying bitcoin since i started honey-badgering him to buy some at $1 back in 2011) have dulled shitcoiners' and nocoiners' minds. the collective unconscious, controlled now by psychotic cats, plays "friends" reruns all day in our heads. our imaginations and inventiveness have been euthanized by the rancid catnip of fiat money. fortunately, bitcoin puts an end to the paper chase manhattan bank money laundering lobotomy. it's a stun gun to the genitals. a monetary defibrillator to the treasure chest. don't believe me? have you seen the president of el salvador trolling the international monetary fund (imf) on twitter? he's taunting them, telling them he's "buying the dip." i tweeted president nayib bukele when he announced he was going to tap his nation's volcanoes to mine $1 billion in bitcoin - that he should sell billions worth of future bitcoin earnings collateralized "volcano bonds" to pay off the imf loan and get rid of those leeches. i tagged mike novogratz in the tweet, figuring he could sell a bunch to his wall street crony pals, but so far he hasn't moved on it. i don't really trust that guy. maybe because he's a shitcoiner at heart. meanwhile, back in el salvador, 42% of the
